{\\\"linearity_ratio\\\": 0.2644, \\\"linearity_ratio_std\\\": 0.0073, \\\"magnitude_ratio\\\": 0.1578, \\\"magnitude_ratio_std\\\": 0.0088, \\\"cosine_similarity\\\": 0.1962, \\\"cosine_similarity_std\\\": 0.0268, \\\"predictor_effect_norm\\\": 482.65, \\\"spon_correction_norm\\\": 76.2, \\\"interaction_norm\\\": 147.74, \\\"num_samples\\\": 50, \\\"seq_len\\\": 2048, \\\"conclusion_type\\\": \\\"weak_subsumption\\\"}, \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"## Subsumption Test Results (50 samples, seq_len=2048)\\\\n\\\\n### 核心指标\\\\n- Linearity ratio: 0.264 ± 0.007 (cross-layer cascade non-linearity)\\\\n- Magnitude ratio: 0.158 ± 0.009 (SPON correction / predictor effect)\\\\n- Cosine similarity: 0.196 ± 0.027 (near-orthogonal)\\\\n\\\\n### Effect magnitudes\\\\n- ‖D(predictor)‖ = 482.65 (KL predictor logit shift)\\\\n- ‖D(SPON)‖ = 76.20 (SPON bias correction logit shift)\\\\n- ‖interaction‖ = 147.74 (cross-layer cascade non-linearity)\\\\n\\\\n### 结论：Informative — SPON corrections are orthogonal and negligibly small\\\\n\\\\n1. **SPON 与 mask 效应正交**：cos_sim ≈ 0.20，两种修正作用在不同维度\\\\n2. **SPON 量级微小**：仅为 predictor 效应的 16%（76 vs 483）\\\\n3. **逐层精确可加**：交互项（148）来源于跨层级联非线性（down_proj 是线性的，层内 mask+bias 严格可加），非层内交互\\\\n4. **支持 D010 \\\\\\\"marginal complement\\\\\\\" framing**：SPON 提供的是正交但微弱的补充，不构成有意义的 compositional gain\\\\n\\\\n⚠️ 不使用 \\\\\\\"subsumption\\\\\\\" 措辞——数据显示正交性而非包含关系。正确表述：SPON corrections are orthogonal to and much smaller than predictor mask effects; the non-zero interaction term arises from cross-layer cascading, not intra-layer coupling.\\\\n\\\\n### 产出文件\\\\nresults/subsumption_test/subsumption_results.json (15KB)\\\", \\\"completed_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-15T20:29:47.949Z\\\"}, intermediates.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    original_forwards = {}\\n\\n    if global_masks is not None:\\n        for layer_idx, layer in enumerate(model.model.layers):\\n            if layer_idx not in global_masks:\\n                continue\\n            original_forwards[layer_idx] = layer.mlp.forward\\n            mask = global_masks[layer_idx].to(device=device,\\n intermediate = af(gp(x)) * up(x)\\n                    return dp(intermediate * m.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0))\\n                return fwd\\n\\n            layer.mlp.forward = make_masked_fwd(\\n                mlp.gate_proj, mlp.up_proj, mlp.down_proj, mlp.act_fn, mask\\n            )\\n\\n    total_loss = 0.0\\n    total_tokens = 0\\n    for ex in examples:\\n        ids =   logits.view(-1, logits.size(-1)), labels.view(-1), reduction=\\\"sum\\\"\\n        )\\n        total_loss += loss.item()\\n        total_tokens += labels.numel()\\n\\n    for layer_idx, fwd in original_forwards.items():\\n        model.model.layers[layer_idx].mlp.forward = fwd\\n\\n    return torch.exp(torch.tensor(total_loss / total_tokens)).item()\\n\\n\\n@torch.no_grad()\\ndef Registry updated. Summary:

## kl_sparsity70_s42_v2 PPL 评估结果

| Metric | Value |
|--------|-------|
| true_dense_ppl | 6.12 |
| **predictor_hard_mask_ppl** | **13.84** |
| teal_sparse_ppl | 136,069.61 (broken) |
| model_level_sparsity | 0.699 |

## 70% vs 50% Sparsity 对比

| | 50% KL (4-seed mean) | 70% KL (seed 42) |
|--|--|--|
| PPL | 12.00 ± 0.07 | 13.84 |
| PPL degradation vs dense | +5.88 (+96%) | +7.72 (+126%) |

**关键发现：**
- 70% sparsity 的 predictor hard mask PPL (13.84) 仅比 50% (12.00) 高 15%，说明 KL predictor 在高稀疏度下仍能有效分配 neuron budget
- Static global topk (teal_sparse) 在 70% sparsity 下完全失效——per-layer sparsity 分布极不均匀 (0.508–0.876)，late layers (L25-28) 被过度剪枝至 85%+ 导致 PPL 爆炸。这表明 70% sparsity 下 **必须用 per-token adaptive masking**，static allocation 不可行
